1b1('bum): @##//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa stong phrag brgya pa/bam po 
1b2('bum): nyis brgya gya gsum pa/bcom ldan 'das de ci'i slad du zhe na/chos kyi dbyings 
1b3('bum): la ni rnam par rtog pa de dag ma mchis te/gzugs kyang chos kyi dbyings ma 
1b4('bum): lags/gzugs las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/gzugs 
1b5('bum): nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang gzugs so//tshor ba 
2a1('bum): @#//yang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/tshor ba las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/tshor ba 
2a2('bum): nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang tshor ba'o//'du shes kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lags 'du shes 
2a3('bum): las gud na yang chos kyi dbyangs ma mchis te/'du shes nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings 
2a4('bum): so//'du byed kyang chos kyi dbyings ma legs/'du byed las gud na yang chos kyi ngabyings ma mchis te/'du byed nyid 
2a5('bum): kyang chos kyi dbyings so/chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang 'du byed do//rnam par shes pa yang chos kyi dbyings ma 
2a6('bum): rnam par shes pa las gud na yang chos kyis dbyings ma mchis te/rnam par shes pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings/
2b1('bum): chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang rnam par shes pa'o//mig kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/mig las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/mig nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//
2b2('bum): chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang mig go/rna ba yang chos kyi dbyings ma lags /rna ba las gud na yang chos kyi 'byings ma mchis te/rna ba nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings bso//chos kyi 
2b3('bum): dbyings nyid kyang rna ba'o//sna yang chos kyi dbyings ma la gas/sna las gud na yang chos kyis dbyings ma mchis te/sna nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang sna'o//lce 
2b4('bum): yang chos kyi dbyings ma lag sa/lce las gud na yang chos kyi byings ma mchis te/lce nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings sa//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi lce'o//lus kyang chos kyi dbyings ma 
2b5('bum): lus las gud na yang chos kyi byangs ma mchis te/lus nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings lo//chos kyi dbyings nyid gyi lus so//yid nyid kyang chos kyi dbyingasam lags/yid las gud 
2b6('bum): na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/yid nyid kyang chos kyi dbyi ngas so//chos kyi dbyings nyid rkyang yid do//gzugs nyid kyang chos kyi dbyangs ma lags/gzugs las gud na yang chos
2b7('bum): kyi dbyings ma mchis te/gzugs nyid kyang chos kyi byings so//chos kyi dbyingasanyid kyang gzugs so//sgra yang chos kyi dbyings ma la gas/sgra las gud na yang chos gyi dbyings ma 
3a1('bum): @#//mchis te/sgra nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang sgra'o//dri yang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/dri las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/dri nyid kyang chos kyi 
3a2('bum): dbyings so//chos kyi dbying nyid kyang dri'o//ro yang chos kyi dbyings ma legs/ro las gud na yang chos kyi 'byings ma mchis te/ro nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid 
3a3('bum): kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lags /reg las gud na yang chos kyis byings ma mchis te/reg nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings su//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi kyang reg go/chos kyang chos kyi dbyings ma la gas/
3a4('bum): chos las gud na yang chos kyi byings ma mchis te/chos nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings swo//chos kyi dbyings nyid ma lags//chos kyi dbyings ma lags
3a5('bum): shes pa las gud na yang chos kyi sbyings ma mchis te/mig gi rnam par shes pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings
3a6('bum): kyi dbyings ma lags/rna ba'i rnam par shes pa las gud na yang tshos kyi dbyings ma mchis te /rna ba'i rnam par shes ba nyid kyang chos kyi dbyingasaso//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang rna ba'i rnam par shes
3a7('bum): pa'o||sna'i rnam par shes pa yang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/sna'i rnam par shes pa las gdu na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis ste/sna'i rnam par shes pa nyid kyi yang chos kyi dbyings so//chos
3a8('bum): kyi dbyings nyid kyang sna'i rnam par shes pa'o//lce'i rnam par shes pa yang tshos kyi dbyings ma lags /lce'i rnam par shes pa las bdud na yang chos kyi dbyings la mchis te/lce'i rnam par
3b1('bum): shes pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang lce'i rnam par shes pa'o//lus kyi rnam par shes pa yang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/lus kyi rnam par shes pa las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings 
3b2('bum): ma mchis te/lus kyi rnam par shes pa'i nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings sa//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi lus kyi rnam par shes pa'o//yid kyi rnam par shes pa yang /chos kyi dbyings ma lags/yid kyi rnam par shes pa
3b3('bum): las gud na yang chos kyi phyings ma mchis te/yid kyi rnam par shes pa nyid kyang chos kyi byings so//chos kyi dbying nyid kyang yid kyi rnam par shes pa'o/mig gi 'dus te reg pa yang chos kyi dbyings mi
3b4('bum): lags/mig gi 'dus te reg ba las gud na yang chos kyis dbyings ma mchis te/mig gi 'dus te reg nyid kyang chos kyi dbying so//chos kyi dbyings nyi kyang mig gi 'dus te reg pa'o//rna ba'i 'dus
3b5('bum): te reg pa yang chos kyi dbyangs ma lags/rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa las gud na yang chos gyi dbyings ma mchis te/rna ba'i 'dus te reg ba nyid kyang chos so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang rna ba'i 'dus te reg
3b6('bum): pa'o//sna'i 'dus te reg pa yang chos
3b7('bum): sna'i 'dus te reg pa'o//lce'i 'dus te reg pa yang cos kyi dbyings ma lags/lce'i 'dus te reg pa las bud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/lce'i 'dus te reg ba nyid kyi kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyigs nyid kyang
3b8('bum): 'di pa nyid kyang lce'i 'dus te reg pa'o//lus kyi 'dus te reg pa yang chod kyi dbyings ma lags/lus kyi 'dus te reg ba las dud na yang chos kyi dbying ma mchis te/lus kyi 'dus ta reg pa nyid kyang chos kyi
4a1('bum): @#//dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'o//yid kyi 'dus te reg pa yang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/yid kyi 'dus te reg pa las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis 
4a2('bum): te/yid kyi 'dus te reg ba nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbying nyid kyang yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'o/mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba yang chos kyi dbyings ma la gas/mig gi 'dus te 
4a3('bum): reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor b.las gud na yang chos kyi 'dus te mig gi 'dus te reg pa'irkyen gyis tshor ba nyid kyang chos kyi dbyi ngas so//chos kyi dbyings 
4a4('bum): nyid kyang mig gi 'dus te reg ba'i rkyen gyis tshor ba'o//rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen kyis tshor ba yang chos kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen
4a5('bum): gyis tshor ba'o//rna ba'i bdus te reg pa'i rkyen gyisatshor ba yang chos kyi dbyi ngas ma lags/rna ba'i 'dus te reg ba'i rkyen 
4a6('bum): gyis tshor ba nyid kyang chos gyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyangs nyid kyang rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa 'i rkyen gyis tshor ba'o/sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyenagyis tshor ba yang chos kyi byings ma mchis te/rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i
4a7('bum): ba'i rkyen gyis tshor ba las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/sna'i 'dus te reg ba'i rkyenagyis tshor ba nyid kyang chosakyi dbyings so//chos kyi byings nyid kyang sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rten gyis tshor ba'o/
4a8('bum): lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen: gyis tshor ba yang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rken gyis tshor ba las gud 
4a9('bum): na yang chos kyi dbyings mamachis te/lce'i 'dus te reg ba'i rken gyis tshor ba nyid 
4a10('bum): kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba'o//lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rgyen gyis tshor ba yang chos kyidabyings ma lags/lus kyi 'dus te reg ba'i rkyen gyis
4b1('bum): tshor ba las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rten gyis tshor ba'o//yid kyi 'dus 
4b2('bum): te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor pa yang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyenagyis tshor ba las gud na yang chod kyi dbyings ma mchis te/yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rgyen gyis tshor ba nyid kyang chas kyi dbyings
4b3('bum): so||chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba'o//sa'i khams kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lags /sa'i khams las gud na yang chos kyi 'byings ma mchis te/sa'i khams nyid kyang chos kyi
4b4('bum): dbyings so//chos kyi dbying nyid kyang sa'i khams so//chu'i khams kyang chos kyi dbyigs ma lags/chu'i khams las gud na yang chas kyi dbyings ma mchis te /chu'i khams nyid kyang chos kyi dbying so//chos kyi
4b5('bum): dbyings nyid kyang chu'i khams so//me'i khams kyang chos kyi dbyangs ma lags/me'i khams las gud na yang cos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/me'i khams nyid kyang chos kyis dbyings so//chos kyi dbyangs nyid kyang 
4b6('bum): me'i khams so//rlung gi khams kyang chos kyi dbying ma lags/rlung gi khams las gud na yang chod kyi dbyings ma mchis te/rlung gi khams nyid kyang chos kyi 'byings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang rlung gi khams
4b7('bum): so||nam mkha'i khams kyang chos kyi dbyi ngas ma lags/nam mkha'i khams las gud na yar chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/nam mkha'i khams nyid kyang chos kyi byings so//chos kyi dbyi ngas nyid kyang nam mkha'i   
4b8('bum): khams so//rnam par shes pa'i khams kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/rnam par shes pa'i khams lam gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchisate/rnam par shes pa'i kham sa kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi bying
5a1('bum): @#//nyid kyang rnam par shes pa'i khams so//ma rig pa yang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/ma rig pa las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/ma rig pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang 
5a2('bum): ma rig pa'o//'du byed kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lags /'du byed las gud na yang chos kyi byings ma mchis te/'du byed kyang chos kyi dbying so//chos kyi dbyings kyang 'du byed do//rnam par shes pa yang chos kyi dbyings
5a3('bum): ma lags|rnam par shes pa las gud na yang chos gyi dbyings ma mchis te/rnam par shes pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyi ngas so//chos kyi dbying sa nyid kyang rnam par shes pa'o//ming dang gzugs kyang chos kyi dbyings ma
5a4('bum): lags/ming dang gzugs las gud na yang chos kyi babyings ma mchis te/ming dang gzugs nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi ming dang gzugs so//skye mched drug kyang chos kyi dbyings ma
5a5('bum): legs/skye mched drug las gud na yang chos kyi dibyings ma mchis te/skye mched drug nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings sa//chos kyi dbyings nyid skye mched drug go/reg pa yang chos kyi dbyings ma lag sa/reg pa las
5a6('bum): gud na yang chos kyi dbyingasam mchis te/reg pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyingasaso/chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang reg pa'o//tshor ba yang chos kyi dbyings ma legs/tshor ba las gud na yang chos kyis dbyings ma mchis te/tshor ba
5a7('bum): nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang tshor ba'o//sred pa yang chos kyi dbyings la gas/sred pa las gud na yang chos kyi/dbyings ma mchis te/sred pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyi ngas so//chos kyi
5a8('bum): dbyings nyid kyang sred pa'o//len pa yang chos kyi dbyings mi lags/len pa las gud na yang chos gyi dbyings ma mchis te/len pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyis so//chos kyi dbyings ny-id kyang len pa'o//srid pa yang
5b1('bum): chos kyi dbyings ma lags/srid pa las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/srid pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang srid pa'o//skye ba yang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/skye ba las gud na phaT 
5b2('bum): chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te skye ba nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings/chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang skye ba'o//rga shi yang chos kyi dbyings ma la gas/rga shi las gud na yang chos kyis dbyings ma mchis te/rga shi nyid kyang chos kyi 
5b3('bum): dbyings so//chos kyi dbyigs nyid kyang rga shi'o//sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yang chos kyi dbyings ma las/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin ba las gud na yang chos kyi 'byings ma mchis te/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyi pa 
5b4('bum): nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings su//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin te//tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yang /chos kyi dbyings ma lags tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu byin pa las gud na yang chos kyi
5b5('bum): dbyings ma mchis te/tshul khrims kyi pha rol du phyin pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyi pa'o//bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa las chos kyi dbyings ma lags/
5b6('bum): bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyinapa las gud na yang chos dbyings ma mchis te bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyinab nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid 
5b7('bum): kyang bzod pa'i pha rol tu yin pa'o//brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa las gud na yang chosakyi dbyings ma mchis te/brtson 'grus kyi pha rol du phyin pa nid kyang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/
6a1('bum): @#//pha rol tu phyin pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin
6a2('bum): pa las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyen pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyangs so//chos kyi dbyings yid kyang shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyi pa'o//nang stong pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbying 
6a3('bum): ma lags/nang stong pa nyid las gud na yang chos kyis dbyings ma mchis te/nang stong pa nyid nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi nang stong pa nyid do//phyi stong pa nyid kyang chos kyis dbyings ma lags/
6a4('bum): phyi stong pa nyid las gud na'ang chos kyi dbyings ma mtshis te/phyi stong pa nyid nyid kyang choskyi dbyings so//chos kyi byings nyid kyang phyi stong pa nyid do//phyi nang stong pa nyid kyang chos
6a5('bum): pa nyid las gud na yang chod kyi dbyings ma mchis te/phyi nang stong pa nyid nyid kyang tshes kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyingsnyid kyang phyi nang stong pa nyid do//stong pa nyid stong pa nyid kyang chos kyi byings ma lags/
6a6('bum): stong pa nyid stong pa nyid las gud nayang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/stong pa nyid stong pa nyidkyang chos kyi dbyings so//choskyi dbyings nyid kyang stong pa nyid stong pa nyid do//chen po stong pa nyid kyang chos kyidbyings
6a7('bum): ma lags/chen po stong pa nyid las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mtshes te/chen po stong pa nyid nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so/chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang chen po stong pa nyid do//don dam pa stong pa nyid kyang chos
6a8('bum): kyi dbyings ma lags/don dam pa stong pa nyid las gud na'ang chos kyi dbyings ma mtshis te/don dam pa stong pa nying nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings/chos kyi dbyings nyid kyad don dam pa stong pa nyid do//'dus byas
6b1('bum): stong pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/'dus byas stong pa nyid las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/'dus byas stong pa nyid nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang 'dus byas stong pa nyid do//
6b2('bum): 'dus ma byas stong pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyangs ma lags/'dus ma byas stong pa nyid las gung na yang chos kyi dbyings la mchis te/'dus ma byas stong pa nying nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi 'dus ma byas 
6b3('bum): stong pa nyid do//mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid kyang chos kyis dbyings ma lags/mtha' las 'das pa stong ba nyid las gud na yang chosakyi dbyings ma mchis te/mtha' las 'das pa stongab nyid kyis chos kyi dbyings so/
6b4('bum): chos kyi dbyings kyang mtha' las 'das pa stong panyid do//thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid kyang chos gyi dbyings ma lags/thog ma dang tha ma med ba stong pa nyid las gud nayang chos kyi dbyings ma mtshis te/thog ma dang 
6b5('bum): tha ma med pa stong pa nyid kyi dbyangs so//chos kyi dbyings kyi dbyangs ma dang tha ma med pa ston pa nyid do//dor ba med pa stong pa nyid kyang /chos kyi dbyings ma lags/dor ba med pa stong pa nyi las gud na yang 
6b6('bum): chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/dor ba med pa stong ba nyid kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang dor ba med pa stong pa nying do//rang bzhin stong pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lags rang bzhin stong pa nyid 
6b7('bum): las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis ste/rang bzhin stong pa nyid kyi dbyings so//chosakyi dbyings nyid kyang rang bzhin stong pa nying do//chos thams cad stong pa nyid kyang chos kyis dbyings ma lags/chos 
6b8('bum): thams cad stong pa nyid las gud na yang chos kyis dbyings ma mchis te/chos thams cad stong pa nid nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi chos thams cad stong pa nying do//rang gi mtshan nyid stong 
7a1('bum): @#//pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/rang gi mtshan nyid stong ba nyid nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang 
7a2('bum): rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa yin do//mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbying ma lags/mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid las gud nas yang chos kyi dbyings ma mtshis te/mi dmigs pa stong ba nyid nyid kyang chos
7a3('bum): kyi dbyings so//chos kyi byings nyid kyang mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid do//dngos po med pa stong pa nyid kyang chos gyi dbyings ma lags/dngos po med pa stong pa nid las gud na yang chos gyi dbyings ma mchis te/dngos po
7a4('bum): med pa stong pa nyid nyid kyang chos kyi dpyings so//chos kyi dbyingasanyid kyang dngos po med pa stong ba nyid do//ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyang chos kyis dbyings ma lags/ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid las gung na yang chos kyi dbying
7a5('bum): ma mchis te/ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid nyid kyang chosakyi dbyings so//chos kyi gdabyings nyid kyang ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid do//dngos
7a6('bum): nyid stong pa nyid las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/dngos bo med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang dngos po med ba'i ngo bo nyid stong ba nyid do//dran pa nye 
7a7('bum): bar gzhag pa rnams kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lags dran pa nye bar gzhag pa rnams las gud na yang chos kyis dbyings ma mchis te/dran pa nye bar gzhag pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyis dbyings nyid
7a8('bum): kyang dran pa nye bar gzhag ba rnams so//yang dag par spong ba rnams kyang chos kyi dbying ma lags/yang dag par spong ba rnas las gud na yang chos gyi dbyings ma mchis te/yang dag par spong pa nyid kyang
7b1('bum): chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang yang dag par spong ba rnams so//rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnam las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis 
7b2('bum): te|rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so/chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams//dbang po rnams kyang chos kyi dbying ma lags/dbang po rnams las gud na yang chos kyi ngabyings 
7b3('bum): ma mchis te/dbang po nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi dbang po rnams so//stobs rnams kyang chos kyi dbyingasam lags/stobs rnams las gud na yang /chos kyi dbyings ma mchis
7b4('bum): te|stobs nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings sa//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyad stobs rnams so//byang chub kyi yan lag rnams kyang chos kyi dbyingsma lags/byang chub kyi yan lag rnam las gud na yang chos
7b5('bum): kyi dbyings ma mchis te/byang chub kyi yan lag nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings //chos kyi dbyings nyid gyad byang chub kyi yan lag rnams//lam yang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/lam las gud na yang chos 
7b6('bum): kyi dbyings ma mchis te /lam nyid kyang chos kyi dbyingasaso//chos kyi dbyings nyid kun la ma mo//'phags pa'i bden pa rnams kyang chos kyi dbying ma lags/'phags pa'i bden pa rnam las gud na 
7b7('bum): yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis ta/'phags pa'i bden pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi 'phags pa'i bden pa rnams//bsam gtan rnams kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lag
7b8('bum): sa bsam gtan rnams las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma ma mchis te/bsam gtan nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyidkyang bsam gtan rnams so//tshad med pa rnams kyang chos 
8a1('bum): @#//kyi dbyings ma lags/tshad med pa rnams las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/tshad med pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang tshad med pa rnams so//gzugs med pa'i 
8a2('bum): snyoms par 'jug pa rnams kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams las gud na yangs chos kyi dbyings ma mchis ste/gzugs med pa'i snyoms 
8a3('bum): par 'jug pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyigs so//chos kyi dbyings dang /gzugs med pa'i snyoms ma mchis te/rnam par thar pa rnams kyang chos kyi dbyangs ma lags/rnam par thar pa rnams las gud na 
8a4('bum): chos kyi dbyings ma mchis 
8a5('bum): te/rnam par thar pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyibs so//chos kyi dbyings ma mchis par thar pa rnams so//mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa dang /rnam par thar pa rnam par 
8a6('bum): gzugs med pa'i snyoms bar 'jug pa rnams las gzugs med pa'i snyoms pa rnams las 
8a7('bum): gzugs med pa'i sgo stong pa rnams las gzugs ma mchis te/rnam par gzugs med pa'i sgo stong ba nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa yang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/rnam par 
8a8('bum): thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med
8a9('bum): pa dang /smon pa med pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang/mtshan ma med pa dang/smon pa med pa'o//mngon par shes pa rnams kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lag sa/mngon par shes pa rnams las gud na yang chos 
8b1('bum): kyi dbyings ma mchis te/mngon par shes pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang mngon par shes pa rnams so//ting nge 'dzin rnams kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/ting nge 'dzin rnams las gud na 
8b2('bum): yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/ting nge 'dzin nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings su gsungs so//chos kyi dbyings nyad kyang ting nge 'dzin rnams so//gzungs kyi sgo rnams kyang chos kyi dbyingasam lags/gzungs kyi sgo rnams las gud 
8b3('bum): na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mtshis te/gzungs kyi sgo nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyan gzungs kyi sgo rnams so//de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs rnams kyang chos kyi dbying ma lags/de bzhin gshegs 
8b4('bum): pa'i stobs rnams las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings mam mchis te/de bzhin gshegs pa'i stob nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings=so//chos kyi dbyings nyid=kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs=rnams so//mi 'jigs pa rnams 
8b5('bum): kyang chos kyi dbyings ma la gas/mi 'jigs pa rnams las gud na yang chas kyi dbyings ma mchis te mi 'jigs pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings
8b6('bum): rig pa rnams kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/so so yang dag par rig pa rnams las gud na yang=chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/so so yang dag par rigs pa nyid kyang chos kyi 'byings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi so so yang dag par 
8b7('bum): dag par rig pa rnams so//byams pa chen po yang chos kyi dbyings ma lags byams pa chen po las gud na yang chos kyis dbyings ma mchis te/byams pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyigs so//chos kyi dbyings nyad kyang byams pa 
8b8('bum): chen po'o//snying rje chen po yang chos kyi dbyibs ma lags/snying rje chen po las gud na yang=chos ma mchis te/snying rje chen po nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbying nyid kyang snying rje chen po'o//sangs rgyas 
9a1('bum): @#//kyi chos ma 'dres pa rnams kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa rnams las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dris pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings 
9a2('bum): so||chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dre pa rnams so//rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu yang chos kyi dbyings ma lags /rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras ba las gud na yang chos kyi byings ma mchis te 
9a3('bum): rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras byi nyid kyang chos kyi dbyingasaso//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras pa'o//lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu yang chos kyi phyir ma lags/lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'dabras 
9a4('bum): bu las gud na yang chos kyi phyir ma mchis te/lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i'abras bu nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid gyang lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'dras bu'o//phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu yang tshos kyi 
9a5('bum): dbyings ma lags/phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bul las gud na yang chos kyis dbyings ma mchis te/phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras ba yang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu'o//dgra bcom pa 
9a6('bum): nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/dgra bcom pa nyid las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/dgra bcom pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//tshos kyi dbyings nyid kyang dgra bcom pa nyid do//rang byang chub kyang chos 
9a7('bum): kyi dbyings ma lags/rang byang chub las gud na yang chos kyi byings ma mchis te/rang byang chub kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang rang byang chub po//lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyigs 
9a8('bum): ma lags/lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nying las gud na yang chos kyi ngabyings ma mchis te/lam gyi rnam pa shes ba nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyi do//rnam pa 
9b1('bum): thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings ma lags/rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid las gud na yang chos kyi dbyings ma mchis te/rnam pa thams cad mkhyenapa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang 
9b2('bum): rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa'i nyid do//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/rab 'byor de de bzhin no//de de bzhin te/ji skad smras pa de bzhin no//gzugs nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyis gzugs 
9b3('bum): so||tshor ba nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings sa//chos kyi dbyings nyid rgyang tshor ba'o//'du shes nyid kyang chos kyi dbying sa so//chos kyi dbyings gnyid kyang 'du shes so//'du byed nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings
9b4('bum): nyid kyang 'du byed do//rnam par shes pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyigs so//chos kyi dbyings yid kyang rnam par shes pa'o//mig nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings su//chos kyi dbyings nyid gyang mig go/rna ba nyid kyang chos kyi dbyangs 
9b5('bum): so||chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang rna ba'o//sna nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings dbyings so//chos kyi dbyangs nyid kyang sna'o//lce nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang lce'o//lus nyid kyang chos kyi dbying
9b6('bum): sa so//chos kyi dbyings nyer kyang lus so//yid nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings 'o//chos kyi dbyings nyid yang yid do//gzugs nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid gzugs so//sgra nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings sa//
9b7('bum): chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang sgra'o//dri nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings sngo//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi dang dri'o//ro nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings
9b8('bum): kyang reg go/chos nyid kyang chos kyi dbying sa so//chos kyi dbyings nid kyang chos so//mig gi rnam par shes pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings
10a1('bum): @#//pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang rna ba'i rnam par shes pa'o//sna'i rnam par shes pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid yang sna'i rnam par shes pa'o//lce'i rnam 
10a2('bum): par shes nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings su//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi lce'i rnam par shes pa'o//lus kyi rnam par shes pa nyid kyangachos kyi dbyings so//chos dbyings nyid kyang lus kyi rnam par shes pa'o//yid kyi rnam 
10a3('bum): par shes pa nyid kyang chos dbyings so//chos kyi dbyigs nyid kyang yid kyi rnam par shes pa'o/mig gi 'dus te reg pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyigs so//chos kyi dbyings ny-id kyang mig gi 'dus te reg pa'o//rna 
10a4('bum): ba'i 'dus te reg pa nyid kyangachos kyi dbyings//chos kyis dbyings nyid kyang rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'o//sna'i 'dus te reg pa nyid kyi dbyings so//chos kyis dbyings nyid kyi sna'i 'dus te reg pa'o/lce'i 
10a5('bum): 'dus te reg pa nyid kyang /chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyangs nyid kyang lce'i 'dus te reg pa'o/lus kyi 'dus te reg pa nyid kyang/chos kyi dbyings so//chos skyid dbyings nyid kyang las kyi 'dus te reg pa'o//yid 
10a6('bum): kyi 'dus te reg pa nyid na kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos la dbyings nyid kyang yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'o/mig gi 'dus te rag pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang mig 
10a7('bum): gi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor pa'o//rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen ni rgyas tshor ba nyid kyang chos gyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang rna ba'i'adus te reg pa'i rkyen gyi tshor ba'o//sna'i 'dus te reg bI 
10a8('bum): rkyen gyis tshor ba yang chos kyi dbyings so/chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi sna'i 'dul te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshol ba'o//lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen kyis tshor ba yang ches kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyangs nyid kyang 
10b1('bum): lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba'o//lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyi sa tshor ba yang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rten gyis tshor ba'o//yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen
10b2('bum): tshor ba yang chos kyi dbying so//chos kyi dbyings nyis kyang yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i skyen gyis tshor ba'o//sa'i khams nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chas kyi dbyings nyid kyang sa'i khams so//chu'i khams nyid kyang chos
10b3('bum): kyi dbyings so//chos kyi ngabyings nyid kyang chu'i khams so//me'i khams nyid kyang chos kyis dbyings so//chos kyi dpyings nyid kyang me'i khams so//rlung gi khams nyid kyang chos kyi dibyangs so//chos kyi dbyings
10b4('bum): rlung gi khams so//nam mkha'i khams nyid kyang chos kyi ngabyings so//chos kyi dbying sa nyid kyang nam mkha'i khams so//rnam par shes pa'i khams nyid kyang chos kyi 'byings so//chos kyi dbying
10b5('bum): sa nyid kyang sred pa'o//len pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbying so//chosakyi dbyings nyid kyang len pa'o//srid pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyigs so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang srid pa'o//skye ba nyid kyang chos kyi dbying
10b6('bum): sa 
11a1('bum): @#//nyid kyang skye ba'o//rga shi nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang rga shi'o//sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings su//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi don yin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'o//
11a2('bum): tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa nyid bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'o /brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ba nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyag tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu byin pa'o//bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyi na ba nyid kyang chos kyi dbyigs so//chos kyi dbyings 
11a3('bum): nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings ni//chos kyi dbyings nyid gyang nang stong pa nyid do//phyi stong pa nyid nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings dbyings so//chos kyi dbyangs nyid kyang phyi stong pa nyid do//phyi nang stong pa nyid nyid kyang chos
11a4('bum): kyi dbyings nyid kyang chen po stong pa nyid do//don dam pa stong pa nyid kyi dbyings so//chos kyis dbyings nyid kyang don dam pa stong pa nyid do//'dus byas stong pa nyid nyid kyang chod kyi dbyings so//chos
11a5('bum): kyi dbyings nyid kyang 'dus byas stong pa nyid do//'dus ma byas stong pa nyid nyid kyag chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi 'dus ma byas stong pa nyid//mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid nyid kyang //chos
11b1('bum): kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid do//thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyengs nyid kyang thog ma dang tha ma med pa steng pa nyid do//ngo ro med pa 
11b2('bum): stong pa nyid nyid kyang chos gyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyi ngas nyid kyang dor ba med pa stong pa nyid do//rang bzhin stong pa nyid nyid kyang chosakyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyangs nyid kyang rang bzhin stong pa nyid do//chos thams cad 
11b3('bum): stong pa nyid nyid kyang /chos kyi dbyings so//chosakyi dbyings nyid kyang chos thams cad stong pa nyid do//rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid nyid kyang chosagyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbangs nyid kyang rang gi mtshan nyid stong par nyid do//
11b4('bum): mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid nyid kyangachos kyi dbyings so//chos:kyi dbyings nyid kyang mi dmigs pa stong pa nyidado//dngos po med pa stong pa nyid nyidakyang chos kyi dbyings so/chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang dngos po med pa 
11b5('bum): stong pa nyid do//ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid nyid kyangachosakyi dbyings so//chos dbyings nyid kyang ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid do//dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid nyis kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi 
11b6('bum): dbyings nyid kyang yang dag par spong ba rnams so//rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so/|chos kyi dbyings nyid kyangardzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa rnams so//dbang po nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings swo//chos kyi 
11b7('bum): dbyings nyidakyang dbang po rnams so//stobs nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang stobs rnams so//byang chub kyi yan lag nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang byang chub kyi 
12a1('bum): @#//yan lag rnams so//'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang 'phags pa'i lam yan lag rgyad pa'o//'phags pa'i bden pa nyid kyang chos 
12a2('bum): kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyangs nyid kyang 'phags pa'i bden pa rnams so//bsam gtan nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings su//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi bsam gtan rnams so//tshad med pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyangs 
12a3('bum): so||chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang tshad med pa rnams so//gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyigs so//chos kyi dbyings kyi dbyangs nyid kyang gzugs med pa'i snyoms pa 'jug pa rnams so//
12a4('bum): rnam par thar pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbying so//chos kyi dbyings nyidakyang rnam par thar pa rnams so//mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa'i dbyings so//chos kyi dbang nyid kyang mthar 
12a5('bum): gyis gnas pa'i snyoms pa 'jug pa dang /rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong ba 
12a6('bum): nyid dang /mtshan ma med ba dang /smon pa med pa'o//mngon par shes pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyingsa so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyi mngon par shes pa rnams so//ting nge 'dzin nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi 
12a7('bum): dbyings nyid kyang ting nge 'dzin rnams so//gzungs kyi sgo nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings sa so//chos kyi dbyings kyi dbyangs kyi sgo rnams so//de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings su//chos kyi 
12a8('bum): dbyings nyidakyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i stob rnams so//mi 'jigs pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbangs so//chos kyi dbyings nid kyang mi 'jigs pa rnams so//so so yang dag par rig pa nyid kyang chos kyi 
12b1('bum): dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang so so yang dag par rig pa rnams so//byams pa chen po nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbying nyid kyang byams pa chen po'o//snying rje chen po nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi
12b2('bum): dbyings nyid kyang snying rje chen po'o//sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dre ba rnams so//rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu nyid kyang chos 
12b3('bum): kyi dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu'o//lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu nyid kyang chos kyi dbyings rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa'i tshe/chos kyi dbyings la ma gtogs pa'i
12b4('bum): 'bras bu nyid kyang chos kyidabyings so//chos kyi dbyigs nyid kyang phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu'o//dgra bcom pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbyigs so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang lan cig phyir 'ong ba'i 'bral bu'o//phyir mi 'ong bI 
12b5('bum): dbyings so//chos kyi dbyings nyid kyang rang byang chub bo//lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid kyang chos kyi dbying so//chos kyi dbyings nyis kyang lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyi do//rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyang chos 
12b6('bum): kyi dbying so//chos kyi dbying sa nyid kyang rnam pa thams cad mkhyen ba nyid do//byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod ba'i tshe/chos kyi dbyings la ma gtogs pa'i 
12b7('bum): chos 'ga' zhig mthong na ni/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub la gnas par yang mi 'gyur te/chos thams cad ni chos kyi dbyings yin pas/de'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po shes 
12b8('bum): rab kyis rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa'i tshe/chos thams cad chos kyi dbyings su shes nas/chos thams cad ming med pa la med gi brdas chos ston te/'di ni gzugs shes bya'o//'di ni tshor ba zhes bya'o//'di ni rung 
13a1('bum): @#//shes zhes bya'o//'di ni 'du byed ces bya'o//'di ni rnam par shes pa zhes bya'o//'di ni mig ces bya'o//'di ni rna ba zhes bya'o//'di ni sna zhes bya'o//'di ni lce zhes bya'o//'di ni lus zhes bya'o//'di ni yid 
13a2('bum): ces bya'o//'di ni gzugs shes bya'o//'di ni sgra zhes bya'o//'di ni dri zhes bya'o//'di ni ro zhes bya'o//'di ni reg ces bya'o//'di ni chos shes bya'o//'di ni mig gi rnam par shes pa zhes bya'o||'di ni rna ba'i rnam par 
13a3('bum): shes pa zhes bya'o//'di na sna'i rnam par shes pa zhes bya'o/'di ni lce'i rnam par shes pa zhes ba'o//'di ni lus kyi rnam par shes pa zhes bya ba'o//'di ni yid kyi rnam par shes pa zhes pa'o//'di ni mig gi 'dus 
13a4('bum): te reg pa zhes bya'o//'di ni rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa zhes bya'o//'dis ni sna'i 'dus te reg pa zhes ba'o//'di ni lce'i 'dus te reg pa zhes pa'o//'di ni lus kyi 'dus te reg pa zhes 
13a5('bum): te reg pa zhes bya'o//'di ni mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba zhes bya'o//'di ni rna pa'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen kyis tshor ba zhes bya'o//'dir ni sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen
13a6('bum): gyis tshor ba zhes bya'o//'di ni rna ba'i 'dus te rig pa'i rkyen gyis tshor bar zhes bya'o//'di ni lus kyi 'dud te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshong ba zhes bya'o//'di ni sna'i 'dus te reg 
13a7('bum): pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba zhes
13a8('bum): shes bya'o//'di ni chu'i khams shes bya'o//'di ni me'i khams shes bya'o//'dir ni rlung gi khams shes bya'o//'di na nam mkha'i khams shes bya'o//'dis ni rnam par shes pa'i khams shes bya'o//
13a9('bum): 'di ni ma rig pa zhes bya'o//'di ni 'du byed ces bya'o//'di ni rnam par shes pa zhes bya'o//'di ni ming dang gzugs shes bya'o//'di ni skye mched drug ces bya'o//'di ni reg pa zhes bya'o//'di ni
13a10('bum): tshor ba zhes 
13b1('bum): bya'o||'di ni sred pa zhes bya'o//'di ni len pa zhes bya'o//'di ni srid pa zhes bya'o//'di ni skye ba zhes bya'o//'di ni rga shi zhes bya'o//'di ni sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa zhes bya'o//'di ni tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zhes 
13b2('bum): bya'o||'di ni bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa zhe bya'o//'di ni brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa zhe bya ba'o//'di ni bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa zhes pa'o//'di ni shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa/zhes bya'o//'di 
13b3('bum): ni nang stong pa nyid ces bya'o//'di ni phyi stong pa nyid ces bya'o//de ni phyi nang stong pa nyid ces pa'o//'di ni stong pa nyid stong pa nyid ces bya'o/'di ni chen po stong pa nyid ces bya'o//'di ni don dam pa stong pa nyid ces bya'o

109b7('bum): 'dus te reg pa mi sbyor ma 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhen/de ni 'di ltar gang sbyor ba'am/dbye bar bya ba' rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa la nga bo nyid med de//sna'i 'dus te reg pa mi sbyor/mi 'byed de/de ci'i phyir/de ni 
109b8('bum): 'di ltar gang sbyar ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'i sna'i 'dus te reg pa la ngo ba nyid med do//lce'i 'dus te reg pa mi sbyar mi 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de na 'di ltar gang sbyar ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'i lce'i 'dus te 
110a1('bum): @#//reg pa la ngo bo nyid med do//lus kyi 'dus te reg pa mi sbyor mi 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'i lus kyi 'dus te reg ba la ngo bo nyid med do/yid kyi 
110a2('bum): 'dus te reg pa mi sbyor ma'i 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhen/de ni 'di ltar gang sbyang ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'i mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba la ngo bo nyid med de//mig gi 'dus te reg pa'i rten gyis tshor ba mi sbyar mi 'byed de/
110a3('bum): de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni'adi ltar gang sbyar ba'am//dbye bar bya ba'i mig gi lus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshol ba la ngo bo nyid med do //rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen kyis tshor ba mi sbyor mi 'byed de//de ci'i phyir 
110a4('bum): zhe na/de ni 'di ltar gangs sbyar ba'am/dbye bar byas ba'i rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rgyan gyis tshor ba la ngo bo ni de med do//sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyi tshor ba mi sbyor mi 'byed/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar 
110a5('bum): gang sbyar ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'i sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyed gyis tshor ba la ngo bo nying med do//lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gis tshor ba mi sbyor mi 'byedade/de ci'i phyir zhe na//de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar ba 'am/
110a6('bum): dbye bar bya ba'i lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyan gyis tshor ba la ngo bo nyid med do//lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyes tshor ba mi sbyor mi 'bye de/de ci'i phyir zhe na/deni 'di ltar gang sbyar ba'am/dbye bar
110a7('bum): bya ba'i lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen pa'i tshor ba la ngo bo nyid me dado//yid kyi 'dus te rag pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba mi sbyor mi 'byad de/de ci'i phyir zhe na /de ni 'di ltar gang sbyang ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'i
110a8('bum): yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyenagyis tshor ba la ngo bo nyidamed de//sa'i khams mi sbyor mi 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar gang sbyarab 'am/dbye bar bya ba'i
110b1('bum): chu'i khams mi sbyor mi 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'i chu'i khams la ngo bo nyid med do//me'i khams mi sbyor mi 'byed do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni'adi ltar 
110b2('bum): gang sbyar ba'am/dbye bar bye ba'i me'i khams la ngo bo nyid med//rlung gi khams mi sbyor mi 'byed/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de nyid de ltar gang sbyar ba'am//dbye bar bya ba'i rlung gi khams la ngo bo nyid med de//
110b3('bum): nam mkha'i khams mi sbyor mi 'byed/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ci'i phyir zhe na//de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar pa'am/dbye bar bya ba'i nam mkha'i khams la ngo bo nyid medado//rnam par shes pa'i khams mi sbyor mi 'byed
110b4('bum): phyir zhe na/de ni 'di lta gang sbyar ba'am/dbye ba bya ba'i rnam par shes pa'i khams la ngo bo nyid de med do//ma rig pa mi sbyor mi 'byed de//de ci'i phyir zhe na//de na 'di ltar gang sbyar ba 'am/dbye bar bya 
110b5('bum): bya'i ma rig pa la ngo bo nyid med do/'du byed mi sbyor mi 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhe na/deni 'di ltar gang sbyar ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'i 'du byed la ngo bo nyid med do//rnam par shes pa mi sbyor mi 'byed de
110b6('bum): de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar ba 'ang dbye bar bya ba'i rnam par shes pa la ngo bo nyid med do //ming dang gzugs mi sbyor mi 'byed de/deni 'di ltar gang sbyar pa 'am/dbye bar bya ba'i
110b7('bum): ming dang gzugs la ngo bo nyid med do/skye mched drug mi sbyor mi 'byed de/deci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'dadi ltar gang sbyar ba 'am//dbye bar bya ba'i skye mched drug la ngo bo nyid med do/reg pa mi sbyor mi 'byed de /
110b8('bum): de ci'i phyir zhe na/de na 'di ltar gang sbyar pa'am/dbye ba bya ba'i reg pa la ngo bo nyid med de//tshor ba mi sbyor mi 'byed de//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni'adi ltar gang sbyar ba'am//dbye bar bya ba'i tshor bas po
111a1('bum): @#//
111b1('bum): pha rol tu phyin pa la ngo bo nyid med do//nang stong pa nyid mi sbyor mi 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'i nang stong pa nyid la ngo bo nyid med do/phyi stong pa nyid mi sbyor mi byed de/de ci'i

111b6('bum): 'dus ma byas stong pa nyid mi sbyor ma 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhes na/de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar ba 'am/dbye bar bya ba'i 'jus ma byas stong pa nyid la ngo po med do//mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid mi sbyor mi
111b7('bum):  'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhe/de ni 'di ltar gang sgyur ba 'am/dbye bar bya ba mtha' las 'das pa stong ba nyid la ngo bo nyid med de//thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid mi sbyor med 'byed de/de ci'i phyir she na/de ni
111b8('bum): 'di ltar gang sbyar ba 'am dbye bar bya ba'i thog ma dang tha ma med pastong pa nyid la ngo bo nyidamed do//dor ba med pa stong pa nyid mi sbyar mi 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar gangs sbyar ba'am/dbye
112a1('bum): @#//bar bya ba'i dor ba med pa stong pa nyid la ngo bo nyid med do//rang bzhin stong pa nyid mi sbyor mi 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'i rang bzhin stong pa nyid 
112a2('bum): la ngo bo nyid med do//chos thams cad stong pa nyid mi sbyor mi byed de/de ci'i phyir zhe na de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar pa'am/dbye bar bya ba'i chos thams cad stong pa nyidal ngo bo nyid med do//rang gi mtshan 
112a3('bum): nyid stong pa nyid mi sbyor mi bskyed de/de ci'i phyir zhe ne/de ni 'di ltar gang sbyang ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'i ngang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid la ngo bo nying med do//me dmigs pa stong pa nyid mi sbyor mi 
112a4('bum): 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhen/de ni 'di ltar gang spyar ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'am mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid la ngo bonyid med do//dngos po med pa stong pa nyid mi sbyor ma 'byed de/de ci'i phyir ce na/de ni 'di 
112a5('bum): ltar gang sbyar ba'am/dbye par bya ba'i dngos po med pa stong pa yid la ngo bo nyid med do/ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid mi sbyor ba 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar ba'am/dbyer bar bya ba'i dngos po med dang po nyid stong 
112a6('bum): pa nyid stong pa nyid la ngo ba nyid med do//dngos po mi dang po'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid ma sbyor mi 'byed de/de ci'i phyir she na de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar pa'am/dbye bar bya ba'i dran pa nye bar gzhag pa bzhil ngo bo nyid med do//
112a7('bum): yang dag par spong ba bzhi mi sbyor mi 'byed de/de ni 'di ltar gang sbyang ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'i yang dag par spong ba ba bzhi la ngo bo nyid med do/rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhi mi sbyor mi 'byed 
112b1('bum): de|de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar ba 'am/dbye bar bya ba'i rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhi la ngo bo nyid med do//dbang po lnga mi sbyor mi 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de na 'di ltar gang sbyar ba 'am /dbye bar 
112b2('bum): bya ba'i dbang po lnga la ngo bo nyid med do/stobs lnga mi sbyor mi 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni de ltar gang sbyar ba 'am//dbye bar bya ba'i stobs lnga la ngo bo nyid med de//byang chub kyi yan lag bdun mi sbyor mi 'byed 
112b3('bum): de|de ci'i phyir zhe na//de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar pa'am/dbye bar bya ba'i byang chub kyi yan lag bdunal ngo bo nyid med do/'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa mi sbyor mi 'byed de//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de 'di ltar gang 
112b4('bum): sbyar ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'i 'phags pa'i lam yan lagabargyad pa la ngo bo nyid med do//'phags pa'i bden pa bzhi mi sbyor mi 'byed de/de de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni'adi ltar gang sbyar ba'am/dbye bar bya pa'i 'phags pa'i 
112b5('bum): bden pa bzhi la ngo bo nyid med do//bsam gtan bzhi mi sbyor mi 'byed do//de ci'i phyir zhe na de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar ba 'am/dbye bar bya ba'i bsam gtan bzhi la ngo bo nyid med de/tshad med pa bzhi mi sbyor mi 'byedade/
112b6('bum): de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'i tshad med pa bzhi la ngo bo nyid med//gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa bzhi mi sbyor mi 'byed/de/de ci'i phyir zhe na//de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar pa'am/
112b7('bum): dbye bar bya ba'i gzugs med pa'i snyoms pa 'jug pa bzhi la ngo bo nyid me de do//rnam par thar pa brgyad mi sbyor mi 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de na 'di ltar gang sbyar ba'am dbye bar bya ba'i rnam par thar pa 
112b8('bum): brgyad la ngo bo nyid med do/mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug par dgu mi sbyor mi 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni'adi ltar gang sbyar ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'i mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyom par 'jug 
113a1('bum): @#//pa dgu la ngo bo nyid med do//rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa mi sbyor mi 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'i 
113a2('bum): rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong ba nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /smin pa med pa la ngo bo nyid med do/mngon par shes pa rnams mi sbyor mi 'byed de/de ji'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di la ltar gang sbyar ba 'am/dbye bar 
113a3('bum): bya ba'i mngon par shes pa rnams la ngo bo nyid med do/ting nge 'dzin rnams mi sbyor mi 'byed do/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de'i 'di ltar gang sbyar ba 'am//dbye bar bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin rnams la ngo bo nyid med de//
113a4('bum): gzungs kyi sgo rnams mi sbyor mi 'byed de de ci'i phyir zhe na/de na 'di ltar gang sbyar ba 'am dbye bar bya ba'i gzungs kyi sgo rnams la ngo bo nyid med du'o//de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu mi sbyor mi 
113a5('bum): 'byed de/da ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni de ltar gang sbyar ba 'am/bye bar bya ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i stob sa rnams la ngo bo nyid med/mi 'jigs pa bzhi mi sbyor mi 'byed de/des ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 
113a6('bum): 'di ltar gang sbyar ba'am/dbye bar bya ba'i mi 'jigs pa rnams la ngo bo nyid du med do//so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi mi sbyor mi 'byed do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar ba'am dbye bar bya 
113a7('bum): ba'i so so yang dag par rig ba rnams la ngo bo nyid med do//snying rje chen po mi sbyor mi 'byed de//de ci'i phyir zhe na//de ni 'di ltar gang sbyar pa'am/dbye bar bya ba'i snying rje chen po la ngo bo nyid med do/sangs 
113a8('bum): rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad mi sbyor mi 'byed de/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni la 'di ltar gang sbyar ba 'am/dbye bar bya ba'i sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres ba rnams la ngo bo nyid myed do//gsol pa 
113b1('bum): bcom ldan 'das chos gang la yang sbyar ba'am/dbye bar bgyi ba'i ngo bo nyid ma mchis na/gang byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos/bslab pa rab kyi ba'i shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ji ltar 
113b2('bum): mngon par 'grub par 'gyur/shes kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la ma bslab par ni/byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen pos/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang 
113b3('bum): rgya bar mi nus so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/shA ra dwa ti'i bu de de bzhin te/de de bzhin no//ji skad du smras pa de bzhin te/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyinapa la ma bslab par/byang chub sems dpA 
113b4('bum): sems dpa' chen pos/bla na medapa yang dag par rdzogs pa'ibyang chub tu mngon par rdzog par 'tshang rgya mi nus te/de yang thabs mkhas pas yin gyi/thabs mi mkhas pas ma yin no//shA ra dwa 
113b5('bum): ti'i bu gal te byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po/shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa la spyod pa'i tshe/chos gang la la zhig gi ngo bo nyid mi dmigs na ni de'i phyir 'dzin par yang 'gyur na ni/'on te 
113b6('bum): mi dmigs na/'di ni gzugs so//'di ni tshor ba'o//'di ni 'du shes so//'di ni 'du byed do//'di ni rnam par shes pa'o zhes bya bar yang ci zhig gzung /'di dag ni skye mched rnams so//'di ni 
113b7('bum): khams rnams so//'di ni rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba'o//'di ni ma rig pa'o//'di ni 'du byed do/'di ni rnam par shes pa'o//'di ni ming dang gzugs so//'di ni skye mched drug go/'di ni reg pa'o//
113b8('bum): 'di ni tshor ba'o//'di nisred pa'o//'di ni len pa'o//'di ni srid pa'o//'di ni skye ba'o//'di ni rga shi'o//'di ni sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'o//'di ni tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//'di ni bzod 
114a1('bum): @#//pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'o//'di ni brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//'dri ni bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//
114b1('bum): dag ni so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi'o//'di dag ni byams pa chen po'o//'di dag ni snying rje chen po'o//'di dag nis sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad do//'di ni rgyun tu zhugs pa'i 'bras bu'o//'di ni lan cig 
114b2('bum): phyir 'ong ba'i 'bras bu'o//'di ni phyir mi 'ong ba'i 'bras bu'o//'chi ni dgra bcom pa nyid do//'di ni rang byang chub po//'di ni lam gyi rnam pa shes pa nyid do//'di ni rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid do zhes bya bar yang ci 
114b3('bum): zhig gzung ste/shA ra dwa ti'i bu shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ni gzung du med pa'o//bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa ni gzugs du med pa'o//brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ni zung du med pa'o//bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin 
114b4('bum): pa ni gzung du med pa'o/tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa gzung du med pa'o//sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa ni gzud du med pa'o//nang stong pa nyid ni gzung du med pa'o/phyi stong pa nyid ni gzung du ma med pa'o//phyi nang stong pa nyid 
114b5('bum): ni gzung du med pa'o/stong pa nyid stong pa nyid ni gzung du byed pa'o//chen po stong pa nyid ni gzung du yod pa'o//don dam pa stong pa nyid ni gzung du myed pa'o//'dus byas stong pa nyid ni gzung du mi med pa'o//'dus ma 
114b6('bum): byas stong pa nyid ni gzugs du med pa'o//mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid ni gzugs su med pa'o//thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid ni gzug du med pa'o//dor ba med pa stong pa nyid ni bzung du med pa'o//rang bzhin 
114b7('bum): stong pa nyid ni gzung du ba med pa'o//chos thams cad stong pa nyid ni gzugs dang du med pa'o//rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid ni gzugs med pa'o//mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid ni gzungs du med pa'o//dngos po mang ba stong 
114b8('bum): pa nyid ni gzugs su med ba'o//ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid ni gzug dang du med pa'o//dngos pa med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid ni bzugs su med pa'o//dran pa nye bar gzhag pa bzhi ni gzugs du med pa'o//yang dag par spong ba 
115a1('bum): @#//bzhi ni gzung du med pa'o//rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhi ni gzung du med pa'o//dbang po lnga ni gzung du med pa'o/stobs lnga ni gzung du med pa'o//byang chub kyi yan lag bdun ni gzung du med 
115a2('bum): pa'o//'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa ni gzung du med pa'o//'phags pa'i bden pa bzhi ni gzung du medapa'o//bsam gtan bzhi ni gzung du med pa'i//tshad med pa bzhi ni gzung du med
115a3('bum): pa'o//gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa bzhi ni gzung du med/rnam par thar pa brgyad ni gzung du med pa'o/mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa'i gzung du med pa'o/rnam par thar 
115a4('bum): pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa ni gzung du med pa 'o//mngon par shes pa rnams ni gzung du med pa'o/ting 'dzin rnams ni gzung du med//gzungs kyi sgo rnams ni gzung du medapa'o//de bzhin gshegs
115a5('bum): pa'i stobs bcu ni gzung du med pa'o//mi 'jigs pa bzhi ni gzung du med pa'o/so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi ni gzung du me dpa'o//byams pa chen po ni gzung du med pa'o/snying rje chen po 
115a6('bum): ni gzung du med pa'o/sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad ni gzung du medapa'o//shA ra dwa ti'i bu de ni gzung du med pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa ste/'di ltar shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'o//
115a7('bum): de la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos bslab par bya ste/de la bslab pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sem sa dpa' chen pos ni bslab pa yang mi dmigs na/shes rab kyi pha 
115a8('bum): rol tu phyin pa lta ci smos/byang chub lta yang ci smos/byang chub kyi chos rnams lta yang ci smos/sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams lta yang ci smros/rang sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams lha yang ci smos
115b1('bum): nyan thos kyi chos rnams lta yang ci smos/so so'i skye bo'i chos rnams lta yang ci smo sa/de ci'i phyir zhe na/shar dwa ti'i bu de ni 'di ltar chos gang la yang ngo bo nyid med do//de ltar chos thams cad dngos po 
115b2('bum): med pa'i ngo bo nyid la so so'i skye bo'i chos ni gang /rgyun tu zhugs pa ni gang zhig yod/lan cig phyir 'ong ba ni gang zhig yod/phyir mi 'ong ba ni gang zhig yod /dgra bcom pa ni gang zhig yod/rang sangs 
115b3('bum): rgyas ni gang zhig yod/byang chub sems dpa' ni gang zhig yod/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas ni gang zhig yod/gal te gang zag de dag dmigs 
115b4('bum): su med na/chos gang gis so so'i skye bo 'am/rgyun tu zhugs pa 'am/lan cig phyir 'ong ba'am/phyir mi 'ong ba 'am/dgra bcom pa 'am/rang sangs rgyas sam/byang chub sems dpa' 'am/
115b5('bum): de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcam pa yang dag par rdzogs ba'i sangs rgyas zhe bya bar brjod pa'i chos de dag 'byung ba yang gal 'gyur////shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa stong phrag brgya pa/bam po nyis 
115b6('bum): brgya gya dgu pa/gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das chos thams cad dngos po ma mchis pa la/'di ni so so'i skye bo zhes bgyi ba nas/'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra pa bcom pa yang dag par rdzos
